NTo, is hereby given that the Fort Zelandia Light, No. 18 in the publishe Chinese Lighthouses for 18go, was discontinedton the 3rd November 1890; and,on*tesm -dae a new sixth order, fixed, dioptrio, whit iIght to lbe known as. the Anping igtwa exhibited upon an iron lattice mast painted white, which stands at a distance of about34Yad tthe N. 5 6&#39; 15&#39; W. from the tree on to of the moend in Fort Z$elandia, The light ilbimninates the are of the horizon from S. 38V W. round by west andnrh oN. 54.W. The bearings anemagnetic and from seaward. The light is elevated 32) feet above the level of the sea, and in clear weatheritsol be visible at a distance of io nautical miles.
